  static float Scale()
  static int MonitorCount()

  static void SetCursorShape(CursorShape shape)
  static void SetIme(bool enabled, float x = 0f, float y = 0f)
  static void SetMaximized(bool maximized)
  static void SetMode(WindowMode mode)
  static void SetPosition(int x, int y)
  static void SetSize(uint width, uint height)
  static void SetStyle(bool decorations = true, bool resizable = true, bool alwaysOnTop = false)
  static void SetTitle(string title)
  static void SetVideoMode(int monitor, int mode)
  static void StartDragMove()
  static void StartDragResize(WindowEdge edge)
readonly struct Bevy.WindowCloseRequested : IEquatable<WindowCloseRequested>
  bool Equals(WindowCloseRequested other)
  override bool Equals(object obj)
  override int GetHashCode()
  override string ToString()
  static bool op_Equality(WindowCloseRequested left, WindowCloseRequested right)
  static bool op_Inequality(WindowCloseRequested left, WindowCloseRequested right)
enum Bevy.WindowEdge : int
  Bottom = 4
  BottomLeft = 5
  BottomRight = 3
  Left = 6
  Right = 2
  Top = 0
  TopLeft = 7
  TopRight = 1
readonly struct Bevy.WindowFocusChanged : IEquatable<WindowFocusChanged>
  WindowFocusChanged(bool Focused)
  bool Equals(WindowFocusChanged other)
  bool Focused { get; init; }
  override bool Equals(object obj)
  override int GetHashCode()
  override string ToString()
  static bool op_Equality(WindowFocusChanged left, WindowFocusChanged right)
  static bool op_Inequality(WindowFocusChanged left, WindowFocusChanged right)
  void Deconstruct(out bool Focused)
enum Bevy.WindowMode : int
  BorderlessFullscreen = 1
  Fullscreen = 2
  Windowed = 0
readonly struct Bevy.WindowPlace : IEquatable<WindowPlace>
  WindowPlace(bool HasPosition, int X, int Y, uint Width, uint Height, bool Maximized)
  bool Equals(WindowPlace other)
  bool HasPosition { get; init; }
  bool Maximized { get; init; }
  int X { get; init; }
  int Y { get; init; }
  override bool Equals(object obj)
  override int GetHashCode()
  override string ToString()
  static bool op_Equality(WindowPlace left, WindowPlace right)
  static bool op_Inequality(WindowPlace left, WindowPlace right)
  uint Height { get; init; }
  uint Width { get; init; }
  void Deconstruct(out bool HasPosition, out int X, out int Y, out uint Width, out uint Height, out bool Maximized)

sealed class Bevy.WithAttribute : Attribute
  Type[] Types { get; }
  WithAttribute(params Type[] types)
sealed class Bevy.WithoutAttribute : Attribute

  WithoutAttribute(params Type[] types)
sealed class Bevy.World : IDisposable
  IReadOnlyCollection<Type> ResourceTypes { get; }
  T GetOrInsertResource<T>(Func<T> factory)
  T GetOrInsertResource<T>(T value)
  T InitResource<T>() where T : new()
  T Resource<T>()
  T? TryResource<T>() where T : class
  World()
  bool ContainsResource<T>()
  bool RemoveResource<T>()
  bool TryGetResource<T>(out T value)
  int ResourceCount { get; }
  void Clear()
  void Dispose()
  void InsertResource<T>(T value)
